# Federation reconciliation register

Author: Codex. Date: 2026-11-10. Planning remediation, not implementation acceptance. This register is normative input to the BDPv4 plan. Source snapshots precede amendments and preserve old decisions without treating them as the current design. Original HV packets remain immutable.

## Census and completion rule

Read both host stores through the Codex MCP plugin; LEGION access used SSH as kingd. Selection includes every record mentioning federation/LocalProxy/DirectProxy/audit, every requirement cited by the prior federation plan/handoff and donor TODOs, plus their direct FR/TR/TEST mappings. This intentionally includes contextual identity and regression obligations so exclusions are explicit. The machine-readable register retains host provenance, full original descriptions and structured ACs.

LAB returned zero use cases; LEGION returned one unrelated Smoke UC UpdateService. No pre-existing federation UC was found in those queried workspaces. U01-U15 are planned workflow identifiers, not fabricated persisted numeric UC IDs. Their complete steps, requirements, phases and expected outcomes are in the walkthrough/contract documents. Canonical UC registration with those flows is a Ph0 planning gate before implementation; absence is visible, not claimed coverage in the live UC store.

LEGION TEST export contained 16 malformed unrelated REPL descriptions. Read-only record-level parsing recovered 531 of 547 records and all federation-selected test records; the 16 failed IDs and raw response hash are retained in the inventory receipt. No source YAML was rewritten. This limits claims about the unrelated full-workspace TEST export, not the enumerated federation scope.

## Prior decisions and donor TODOs

- D1 path normalization before migration: retain in Ph0/Ph2. Syntax-aware paths, forward-slash storage, foreign-path rejection and aliases precede import; no unreviewed wholesale donor merge.
- D2 signed backfill envelopes/no fanout: preserve integrity, idempotency and no business-effect replay; replace transport for versioned mode with Dolt history plus IdentityServer gateway and independent audit delivery (Ph3/Ph5/Ph6). Legacy HMAC remains only in explicit legacy mode.
- D3 hub-owned CurrentRequirementLayerKey: retain protected hub-admin policy and drift reporting. Heartbeats cannot overwrite it. Host RunAs/paths/tunnels stay local; portable settings travel only as explicit version commits (Ph2/Ph5).
- D4 temporary local reads then hub read-switch: superseded by operator 2026-11-10 autonomy. All ordinary reads/writes remain local before and after synchronization (Ph2).
- D5 explicit path-rekey maintenance, backup/dry-run/report: retain for legacy source preparation. No startup rewrite or blind rekey of historical text (Ph2/Ph3).
- D6 transactional reference integrity and revalidated FKs: retain for legacy rekey/migration; new cross-store links use explicit validators rather than impossible cross-database FKs (Ph2/Ph3).
- D7 no dual filesystem-key fallback: retain syntax-normalized path identity and startup guard. Permanent business-record aliases are a different scope and required by current review; they do not restore ambiguous path fallback (Ph2/Ph3).
- 2026-10-13 full session snapshot decision: retain every field/child, PlanFile/TodoId, sanitizer parity, local-only snapshot construction and explicit version behavior. Transfer automatic audit unversioned; explicitly promoted snapshots may be project-versioned (Ph2/Ph3/Ph5).
- 2026-10-19 MCP-FEDIDENTITY-001: retain existing integrated IdentityServer, user management on federation hosts, single-user nodes and hub authentication. Machine tokens do not establish human identity; bind node client to authenticated owner (Ph2/Ph5/Ph7).
- 2026-10-20 audit-to-hub decision, reiterated in current review: amend local-only audit policy. Permanent local ledgers push append-only with idempotent IDs/receipts; TODO audit history is included in backfill (Ph2/Ph3/Ph5).
- Later keyserver scope decision FR-MCP-173: supersedes general-federation coordinator gating in FR-MCP-125/TR-MCP-TXNCOMPAT-001. Preserve genuine QuadBrain gating and legacy HMAC separation (Ph2/Ph6).
- 2026-11-10 autonomy, reuse, creator tags, v1 branches and explicit commits: controlling design. Pull requests remain deferred; Dolt is the requested fourth provider; data migration tool is required.
- Old baseline-relative test tolerance: historical only. Current Byrd gate requires zero failures and zero skips; donor evidence is not a new phase-exit pass.
- DEPLOY HOLD: remains in effect. Ph8 requires explicit operator release, exact rollback boundary, one-node 48-hour soak and SSH kingd; no deployment performed.
- MCP-FEDIDENTITY-001: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7. Preserve applicable behavior; supersede conflicting transport/read-switch rules as above. Original description and implementation tasks retained in inventory JSON; not marked implemented.
- IMPL-FEDBACKFILL-001: Ph3 seed; Ph6 join. Preserve applicable behavior; supersede conflicting transport/read-switch rules as above. Original description and implementation tasks retained in inventory JSON; not marked implemented.
- IMPL-FEDPATH-001: Ph0/Ph2/Ph3. Preserve applicable behavior; supersede conflicting transport/read-switch rules as above. Original description and implementation tasks retained in inventory JSON; not marked implemented.
- IMPL-FEDROUTE-001: Ph2/Ph5. Preserve applicable behavior; supersede conflicting transport/read-switch rules as above. Original description and implementation tasks retained in inventory JSON; not marked implemented.
- IMPL-FEDWSSYNC-001: Ph2/Ph5. Preserve applicable behavior; supersede conflicting transport/read-switch rules as above. Original description and implementation tasks retained in inventory JSON; not marked implemented.
- PLAN-PATHNORM-001: Ph0/Ph2/Ph3. Preserve applicable behavior; supersede conflicting transport/read-switch rules as above. Original description and implementation tasks retained in inventory JSON; not marked implemented.

## Requirement and acceptance-criterion dispositions

### FR-MCP-026

Disposition: Retain existing identity/GitHub capability. OAuth-provider federation is contextual, not new database federation scope.. Owner: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7. Coverage: U01,U06; RevokedNodeCannotPublish; explicit DirectProxy compatibility.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-026 -> TR-MCP-AUTH-001; FR-MCP-026 -> TR-MCP-AUTH-002; FR-MCP-026 -> TR-MCP-AUTH-003.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: OIDC Authentication. Original obligation: The server shall support standards-based OIDC JWT Bearer authentication for management endpoints using a configurable open-source .NET OIDC provider. Optional external identity federation (for example, GitHub) may be configured through that provider. Management endpoints (agent mutations) require JWT; read endpoints use existing API key auth.

**Covered by:** `OidcAuthOptions`, `Program.cs`, `AgentController`, `Setup-McpKeycloak.ps1`, `setup-mcp-keycloak.sh`
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

### FR-MCP-061

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate. Coverage: Cross-cutting obligation retained with its existing canonical test mapping; no new feature scope.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-061 -> TR-MCP-LOG-002; FR-MCP-061 -> TR-MCP-TODO-003.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Canonical TODO and Session Identifier Conventions. Original obligation: The server shall enforce canonical identifier conventions for newly created TODO and session log payloads:

- Persisted TODO IDs must match either uppercase kebab-case ending in a three-digit sequence suffix (for example `PHASE0-REMOTE-001` or `MCP-TODO-CREATE-001`) or `ISSUE-{number}` for canonical GitHub-backed TODOs.
- Create requests may use `ISSUE-NEW` only as a temporary server-side alias for immediate GitHub-backed TODO creation; persisted TODO IDs must still be canonical.
- Session IDs must match `<Agent>-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>-<suffix>` and be prefixed by the exact `sourceType`/`agent`.
- Request IDs must match `req-<yyyyMMddTHHmmssZ>-<slugOrOrdinal>`.

Validation failures return client-visible errors without mutating persisted data.

**Covered by:** `TodoValidator`, `TodoService`, `SqliteTodoService`, `TodoCreationService`, `SessionLogIdentifierValidator`, `SessionLogController`, `SessionLogService`
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Immediate GitHub-Backed TODO Creation. Original obligation: The server shall support a create-time TODO identifier of `ISSUE-NEW` that immediately creates a GitHub issue, determines the resulting issue number, and persists the local TODO using the canonical `ISSUE-{number}` identifier returned by GitHub.

This behavior shall be available through all server-side TODO creation entry points that already support normal TODO creation. Callers shall receive the canonical persisted identifier rather than the temporary `ISSUE-NEW` alias.

**Technical Implementation:** [TR-MCP-TODO-003](./Technical-Requirements.md#tr-mcp-todo-003) | [TR-MCP-GH-005](./Technical-Requirements.md#tr-mcp-gh-005) | [Mapping](./TR-per-FR-Mapping.md)

**Covered by:** `TodoCreationService`, `GitHubCliService`, `TodoController`, `FwhMcpTools`, `VoiceConversationService`

### FR-MCP-077

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7. Coverage: U01,U06; RevokedNodeCannotPublish; explicit DirectProxy compatibility.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-077 -> TR-MCP-CFG-007; FR-MCP-077 -> TR-MCP-SEC-004.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Server Federation and Request Proxying. Original obligation: The server shall support an opt-in federation mode that proxies incoming requests to a configured remote MCP server instance. Routing shall support a global default target and per-workspace overrides. The feature shall include anti-loop protection (via `X-Mcp-Federation-Hop` header with a configurable maximum hop count), transparent SSE/streaming forwarding for `/mcp-transport`, a runtime management REST API at `/qbrainai/federation`, and auto-discovery of federation targets from running tunnel providers.


**Technical Implementation:** `FederationOptions`, `FederationRegistry`, `FederationProxyService`, `FederationMiddleware`, `FederationController`

**Configuration:** `Mcp:Federation:Enabled`, `Mcp:Federation:Targets`, `Mcp:Federation:DefaultTarget`, `Mcp:Federation:WorkspaceRoutes`, `Mcp:Federation:MaxHops`
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

### FR-MCP-103

Disposition: Amended to current direction; implementation pending. Owner: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7. Coverage: U01,U06; RevokedNodeCannotPublish; explicit DirectProxy compatibility.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-103 -> TEST-MCP-136; FR-MCP-103 -> TEST-MCP-MEMORY-FED-001; FR-MCP-103 -> TEST-MCP-SESSIONLOG-FED-001; FR-MCP-103 -> TR-MCP-FED-001; FR-MCP-103 -> TR-MCP-FED-MEMORY-001; FR-MCP-103 -> TR-MCP-FED-SESSIONLOG-001.
Current contract: Enrolled nodes execute and persist authorized business work locally while offline and exchange explicit version commits through LAB-OMARCHY. The hub coordinates project enrollment, guarded publication and reconciliation; it is not the executor of ordinary node requests. DirectProxy remains an explicitly selected legacy capability under FR-MCP-077. LocalProxy forwarding, outage command replay and read-switch are superseded for versioned federation.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Hub-and-Spoke Federation. Original obligation: The server shall support hub-and-spoke federation where a Hub instance is authoritative for enrolled LocalProxy servers, global workspace inventory, operation intake, sync fanout, queue status, and conflicts. Existing point-to-point federation remains supported as DirectProxy; Standalone continues to serve only local workspaces. A LocalProxy shall forward MCP requests to the configured hub, queue mutating requests durably during hub outages, replay queued operations when connectivity returns, and expose role, hub URL, proxy id, queue depth, stale/queued status, and conflict status to agents and operators.

- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-2 [original satisfied=False]: LocalProxy live forwarding continues to route /qbrainai/* requests, including /qbrainai/memory, through existing proxy middleware when the hub is reachable. Disposition: Superseded for versioned mode by local execution/explicit history; retain only explicitly scoped legacy regression. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-3 [original satisfied=False]: Mutating LocalProxy requests that are eligible for replay are queued durably during hub outages with operation id, domain, resource id, workspace id, headers, body, and base version. Disposition: Superseded for versioned mode by local execution/explicit history; retain only explicitly scoped legacy regression. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-4 [original satisfied=False]: Queued operations replay through signed envelopes when signing is configured. Disposition: Superseded for versioned mode by local execution/explicit history; retain only explicitly scoped legacy regression. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-5 [original satisfied=False]: Stale base-version operations create federation conflicts and do not overwrite authoritative hub state. Disposition: Preserved intent in amended contract/GWT; source satisfaction flag is historical, not carried as new proof. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph5/Ph7.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Keyserver trust service. ### FR-MCP-143

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate. Coverage: Cross-cutting obligation retained with its existing canonical test mapping; no new feature scope.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-143 -> TEST-MCP-194; FR-MCP-143 -> TEST-MCP-REPL-040; FR-MCP-143 -> TR-MCP-CLIENT-001; FR-MCP-143 -> TR-MCP-HEALTH-003; FR-MCP-143 -> TR-MCP-REPL-018; FR-MCP-143 -> TR-MCP-REPL-019; FR-MCP-143 -> TR-MCP-REPL-020; FR-MCP-143 -> TR-MCP-SYNC-001.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Session-log turns persist without a title and never lose a refined one. Original obligation: A session-log turn shall persist end to end when its title is deliberately omitted, and a title the agent has refined shall never be overwritten by a stale local copy. Thirteen open triage reports (BUG-TRIAGE-086 through 101, excluding the already-closed 097) collapse into five root causes, of which two account for eleven of the reports. First, the plugin PowerShell core rejects an empty title at parameter binding even though TR-MCP-REPL-015 requires callers to omit the title so a stale cached one is not resubmitted, so appendDialog, appendActions, completeTurn and the supersede path all fail. Second, the typed client posts anonymous request bodies through a source-generated JSON context that has no metadata for compiler-generated types, so the dedicated retitle endpoints throw before reaching the server. The remaining three are the supersede path clobbering a server-refined title from the local cache, failTurn refusing to close a turn the plugin cache shows as active, and a storage outage leaving health reporting Healthy while every persistence call fails behind inconsistent error shapes.
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Keyserver signs QuadBrain transactions only. Original obligation: Keyserver manifest signing SHALL be used only for QuadBrain transactions: publisher party id prefix brain-slot:, or operation name prefix brain-slot. or quadbrain. All other first-party mutations (TODO, requirements, session-log for every source type including QBAgent, memory, repo, prompt templates, tool registry, GitHub, GraphRAG, voice, agent pool, issue-todo-sync, tool buckets, REPL TODO workflow, requirements ingest, context rebuild, and federation apply/control) SHALL persist without calling ITurnTransactionCoordinator or the keyserver even when Mcp:TurnTransactions:Enabled=true and RequiredForMutations=true. Do not disable the live TurnTransactions flag as the fix. QuadBrain brain-slot.invoke and brain-slot.weight-update remain coordinator-gated and may call keyserver.

- ac-2: brain-slot.invoke and brain-slot.weight-update still invoke the coordinator and may call SignManifestAsync.
- ac-3: Coordinator degraded state does not block general-agent first-party mutations.
- ac-4: Live TurnTransactions.Enabled remains true for QuadBrain; the repo default may stay false.

### FR-MCP-FED-BACKFILL-001

Disposition: Amended to current direction; implementation pending. Owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph6. Coverage: U10,U14; ThreeSourceProviderRoundTrip; MigrationJoinPreservesCommonAncestor.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-FED-BACKFILL-001 -> TEST-MCP-FED-BACKFILL-001; FR-MCP-FED-BACKFILL-001 -> TR-MCP-FED-BACKFILL-001.
Current contract: Inventory every prior domain: TODO and children/links/doc metadata/audit; complete session graph; requirements/AC/mappings/scope layers; memory/versions; use cases/actors; source documents and derived chunks; triage; tools/buckets; agents; brain slots; handoff. Each maps to versioned data, hub-bound unversioned audit, host-local operational state or regenerable derived state with explicit counts/reason. Seed/join replace signed command backfill for versioned mode. Reads and writes always remain local; no read-switch.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: LocalProxy backfills existing workspace data to the hub after enrollment. Original obligation: After enrollment a LocalProxy must copy all existing workspace-scoped data for each enrolled workspace to the hub, idempotently, preserving ids, versions, timestamps and history where the domain supports it, using batched signed envelopes (decision D2). AC1: Domains todo (items, tasks, list items, requirement links, document metadata, audit history), session_log (full graph), requirements (acceptance criteria, traceability, scope layers), memory (+versions), use cases, documents/chunks, triage, tools buckets/definitions, agents, brain slots, handoff runs, actors are each backfilled or explicitly listed as local-only with a reason. AC2: Re-running creates no duplicates and reports already_applied counts. AC3: Backfill operations do not fan out to other proxies. AC4: federation/status reports backfill state per workspace and domain; staleReadStatus is non-clear until complete; while a workspace is incomplete the proxy serves its reads locally and forwards writes (decision D4). AC5: Per-domain per-workspace row-count and checksum parity report after completion. Evidence 2026-10-06: ~1.885M proxy rows vs 1,650 hub rows for the enrolled proxy's workspaces.
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Request workspace paths are interpreted by their own syntax. Original obligation: A server must interpret any request-supplied workspace path by its own syntax (drive letter, UNC, POSIX root), never by the host OS, so a Linux host accepts and stores Windows paths unchanged in meaning.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC1 [original satisfied=False]: On a Linux host, C:\Users\fixture-user, C:/Users/fixture-user resolve to the same Windows identity; nothing is prefixed with the process cwd. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC2 [original satisfied=False]: On a Windows host, /home/x stays a POSIX path. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC3 [original satisfied=False]: Triage submit/status round-trips: getReport with the submitter's original path finds the report on a Linux hub. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC4 [original satisfied=False]: WorkspaceService.Init never creates a directory from a foreign-platform path. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC5 [original satisfied=True]: When the workspace path belongs to the other platform and no Mcp:GitHub:Repository is configured, a GitHub CLI operation never starts gh and returns a failure that names the foreign workspace and the missing Mcp:GitHub:Repository setting; with a repository configured, gh runs with --repo and no working directory (operator 2026-10-29: "gh foreign-workspace AC (Recommended)"; holistic review findings SRC-1 and SRC-4). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: Syntax-based interpretation of request-supplied workspace paths. Original obligation: A server must interpret any request-supplied workspace path by its own syntax (drive letter, UNC, POSIX root), never by the host OS, so a Linux host accepts and stores Windows paths unchanged in meaning and a Windows host accepts POSIX paths. AC1: On a Linux host, C:\Users\kingd and C:/Users/kingd resolve to the same Windows identity and nothing is prefixed with the process working directory. AC2: On a Windows host, /home/x stays a POSIX path. AC3: Triage submit/getReport/queryGroups round-trip on a Linux hub using the submitter's original Windows path. AC4: WorkspaceService.Init never creates a directory from a foreign-platform path. Evidence: TriageService.cs:1417 Path.GetFullPath produced /opt/mcpserver/app/C:\Users\kingd on hub LAB-OMARCHY (2026-10-06). Plan: docs/plans/2026-10-07-federation-fixes.md (P1).

### FR-MCP-FED-ROUTE-001

Disposition: Amended to current direction; implementation pending. Owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5. Coverage: U01,U06; DifferentHostPathsSameProject; UnauthorizedProjectDenied.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-FED-ROUTE-001 -> TEST-MCP-FED-ROUTE-001; FR-MCP-FED-ROUTE-001 -> TR-MCP-FED-ROUTE-001.
Current contract: Current versioned control/remote endpoints resolve immutable ProjectId and authenticated NodeId membership; local paths are host-specific mappings, never authority or fallback to the hub primary workspace. Preserve the old proxy-header routing contract only for explicitly configured legacy DirectProxy paths.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: Hub resolves forwarded requests to the proxy workspace and fails closed. Original obligation: The federation hub must resolve a request forwarded by a LocalProxy to that proxy workspace's canonical row using (X-Mcp-Proxy-Id, proxy-local workspace path) or the GlobalWorkspaceId. AC1: Forwarded GET /todo with X-Workspace-Path F:\GitHub\McpServer from PAYTON-LEGION2 returns that workspace's data (200), never 400 and never the hub primary workspace. AC2: X-Workspace-Path set to the GlobalWorkspaceId resolves to the same workspace. AC3: A request carrying X-Mcp-Federation-Hop without a resolvable workspace fails closed with a 4xx diagnostic and never falls back to the API key's default workspace. AC4: The LocalProxy forwards the hub GlobalWorkspaceId (not its local path) in X-Mcp-Global-Workspace-Id. AC5: The inter-server hub token can only address workspaces registered to the presenting proxy. Evidence 2026-10-07 hub probe: no header -> 200 hub McpServer TODOs (58); proxy path, hash id and forwarded headers -> 400 Unknown workspace path.

Current contract: Version portable project configuration through explicit commits; retain host RunAs, executable paths, DataDirectory, tunnel settings and local filesystem mappings locally. CurrentRequirementLayerKey remains protected hub-admin-owned policy: heartbeat cannot overwrite it and drift is visible. Local offline work uses the last authorized policy. No blanket host-configuration replication.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: Proxy workspace configuration propagates to hub canonical rows. Original obligation: Proxy-owned workspace configuration (Name, IsEnabled, TodoPath, RunAs, AgentPath, PromptTemplate, StatusPrompt, ImplementPrompt, PlanPrompt, IsPrimary, DataDirectory, TunnelProvider) must propagate to the hub canonical Workspaces row on enroll and heartbeat. Hub-maintained state (CurrentRequirementLayerKey) is hub-owned (decision D3): never overwritten by heartbeats, drift reported. AC1: After enroll, every proxy-owned field on every canonical row equals the proxy row (lab repro 55/55). AC2: A TodoPath change on the proxy is visible on the hub after the next heartbeat. AC3: GET federation/workspaces exposes a drift indicator for hub-owned fields that differ from the proxy. AC4: Older proxies that send no configuration fields keep current behaviour. Evidence 2026-10-06: 1 TodoPath and 8 CurrentRequirementLayerKey mismatches of 55; EnsureFederatedWorkspaceRow hard-codes TodoPath docs/todo.yaml.

LocalProxy forwarding and signed command replay are superseded for this mode.

### FR-MCP-PATHNORM-001

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5. Coverage: U01,U06; DifferentHostPathsSameProject; UnauthorizedProjectDenied.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-PATHNORM-001 -> TEST-MCP-PATHNORM-001; FR-MCP-PATHNORM-001 -> TR-MCP-PATHNORM-001.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Persist workspace paths with forward slashes and platform tag. Original obligation: Operator directive (PLAN-PATHNORM-001, decision D1 2026-10-07: delivered in P1). Persisted workspace paths, storage keys and identity hashes use '/' as the separator for all platforms; the windows: (case-insensitive) / case-sensitive: tag is kept; Windows-origin paths are detected by drive letter (C:/) or UNC (//server/share), not by separator; the UNC leading '//' is preserved; paths are converted to '\' only at OS boundaries that require it (\\?\ long-path prefixes, cmd.exe arguments, native APIs). AC1: C:\Users\example, C:/Users/example and c:/users/EXAMPLE produce one storage key. AC2: \\srv\share\x and //srv/share/x produce one storage key that still starts with //. AC3: Existing stores are converted by the --workspace-path-rekey maintenance command (decisions D5-D7 2026-10-07): after it runs no Windows-shaped workspace value contains a backslash, per-table and per-workspace row counts are unchanged, and foreign keys validate; the server refuses to start against an unconverted store (no dual-key lookup). AC4: Native file operations on Windows receive backslash paths where the OS requires them.
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

AC1: C:\Users\kingd, C:/Users/kingd and c:/users/KINGD produce one storage key. AC2: \\srv\share\x and //srv/share/x produce one storage key that still starts with //. AC3: Existing stores are converted by the --workspace-path-rekey maintenance command (decisions D5-D7 2026-10-07): after it runs no Windows-shaped workspace value contains a backslash, per-table and per-workspace row counts are unchanged, and foreign keys validate; the server refuses to start against an unconverted store (no dual-key lookup). AC4: Native file operations on Windows receive backslash paths where the OS requires them.

### FR-MCP-QBOPENAI-001

Source LAB-OMARCHY: QuadBrain OpenAI-compatible chat-completions endpoint. Original obligation: QuadBrain SHALL expose an inbound OpenAI-compatible chat-completions endpoint (chat/completions shape) backed by QuadBrain orchestration, accepting standard OpenAI chat messages and tool/function definitions and returning OpenAI-shaped completions - including assistant tool_calls when the model elects to invoke a tool - so any OpenAI-compatible client, including QBAgent, can use QuadBrain as a drop-in model. QBAgent (FR-MCP-QBAGENT-001) consumes this endpoint as its IChatClient and executes the emitted tool calls via the Microsoft Agent Framework loop; the ACID tightly-coupled profile is NOT required for QBAgent.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-1 [original satisfied=True]: An inbound OpenAI chat request maps the role-tagged transcript onto QuadBrain orchestration and returns the Arbiter output as the assistant message. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-2 [original satisfied=True]: Tool definitions in the request flow through and the response emits assistant tool_calls when QuadBrain elects to call a tool. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-3 [original satisfied=True]: Bearer-auth (Authorization Bearer with X-Api-Key fallback) is validated via WorkspaceTokenService; invalid or missing yields 401. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-4 [original satisfied=True]: QBAgent wires a standard OpenAI IChatClient to the v1 endpoint and runs the Agent Framework tool loop with action tools under the non-ACID profile. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/ac-5 [original satisfied=True]: With no tools in the request and no tool_choice, an Arbiter reply that is not an arbiter envelope is the final answer after one orchestration round and is returned as the assistant message with finish reason stop; an explicit continue envelope still asks for another round (PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 B13; operator decision 2026-10-15 "Both rules (Recommended)"). A tool_choice of auto with no tools counts as no tool_choice, and a committed Arbiter output that is empty is answered with the no-decision message after one round (operator 2026-10-29: "QuadBrain ACs (Recommended)"; holistic review finding SRC-3). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/ac-6 [original satisfied=True]: When the round budget (8 orchestration rounds) ends without a final envelope, the response carries the last Arbiter output formatted for display as the assistant message with finish reason stop, never empty content; a last output whose display formatting leaves nothing (an Arbiter envelope without displayable content, or any other output that formats to nothing), and a budget that ends after an internal-tool round, are answered as ac-7 states (PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 B13; operator decision 2026-10-15 "Both rules (Recommended)"; operator 2026-10-30: "F2: ac-6 vs ac-7 (Recommended)"; operator 2026-11-02: "Strengthen ac-6 (Recommended)"). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/ac-7 [original satisfied=True]: The assistant content is never an Arbiter envelope without displayable content: when display formatting of the answer leaves nothing, the content is a plain message naming the round budget and the last intent, and when the round budget ends after an internal-tool round, the content is that round's internal tool results (operator 2026-10-29: "QuadBrain ACs (Recommended)"; holistic review finding SRC-2). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.

### FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph6/Ph7. Coverage: U10,U13; FullProjectSessionGraphPreserved; AuditBackfillVerified.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001 -> TEST-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006; FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001 -> TR-MCP-SESSIONLOG-006.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Session turns record current plan file and MCP TODO id. Original obligation: Every session-log turn SHALL store planFile and todoId. After persist, query/get SHALL return both fields. They are never null in API output. When no plan or TODO is active, the stored value SHALL be the exact sentinel None (case-sensitive). The first persist of a turn SHALL reject omitted, null, empty, or whitespace planFile or todoId. planFile SHALL accept a workspace-relative path, an exact absolute path, or a ~/ home-relative path. .. is rejected. Query SHALL support exact filters on planFile and todoId, and text search SHALL match those fields. Existing rows SHALL be backfilled from turn contents and agent history under ~. Import, transcript ingest, and federation apply SHALL persist a validated pair (None if extraction finds nothing). Children: AC-FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001-001, AC-FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001-002, AC-FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001-003, AC-FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001-004, AC-FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001-005, AC-FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001-006, AC-FR-MCP-SESSIONLOGCTX-001-007.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: BUG-TRIAGE-139 fourteenth-review safe durable remediation. Original obligation: Use-case creation and its supporting workspace, federation, requirements-export, timeout, and repository-evidence paths shall preserve one canonical ASCII-normalized durable identity, resist filesystem races through handle-relative contained mutation, remain physically bounded and cancellable, preserve exact provider state and real failures, reject blocking special files, and produce hermetic truthful evidence without regressing previously approved controls.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-1 [original satisfied=False]: Persisted workspace, federation, and creation-receipt identifiers normalize Unicode dash variants to canonical ASCII before hashing, and replay is idempotent on SQLite, SQL Server, and PostgreSQL. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-2 [original satisfied=False]: Requirements rollback deletion, restoration writes, replacement, and directory creation are relative to pinned contained directory handles so concurrent junction or reparse swaps cannot affect objects outside the workspace. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/source-ordinal-3 [original satisfied=False]: Mapped-drive and UNC physical identity resolution used by saves and federation upserts is asynchronous, cancellable, finite, and leaves no abandoned workers or repeated uncancellable probes. ### TEST-MCP-102

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate. Coverage: Cross-cutting obligation retained with its existing canonical test mapping; no new feature scope.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Test TEST-MCP-102. Original obligation: Given the provider-factory, native encryption, and maintenance-command workstreams, when SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server are configured for clean-database integration runs, then provider-specific migrations apply successfully and the live encryption state matches the configured state. When encryption is enabled or disabled on an existing database, then the provider-specific transition workflow shall expose a no-data-loss maintenance procedure and dry-run plan before mutation, with automated dry-run tests covering SQLite SEE, PostgreSQL `pg_tde`, and SQL Server TDE command generation. SQL Server provider and migration integration coverage shall use a self-managed SQL Server LocalDB instance created and torn down by the test harness where LocalDB is installed, and otherwise a temporary SQL Server 2025 Developer instance the harness starts for the run and removes afterwards (operator decision 2026-10-15: "allow a temporary sql server 2025 dev instance when necessary."), while SQL Server TDE validation shall run against a separate non-LocalDB SQL Server target because LocalDB cannot validate TDE.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-001 [original satisfied=False]: SqlServerSandboxPlanAssertion_AcceptsLocalDbOrTemporaryInstanceAndRejectsSharedOrNone, then the harness itself: the SQL Server cases use a self-managed LocalDB instance when SqlLocalDB is installed and otherwise a temporary SQL Server 2025 Developer instance the harness starts for the run and removes afterwards, each case on its own uniquely named database that is dropped; a shared server, no server, and a skip are rejected (PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 B11, triage-report-ead2fdaa). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-002 [original satisfied=False]: SqlServer_CleanDatabase_AppliesMigrationsAndPersistsEntity (renamed from SqlServer_LocalDb_CleanDatabase_AppliesMigrationsAndPersistsEntity), HandoffIngestionStorageMigrationTests.SqlServer_HandoffMigration_DowngradeAndReupgrade and HostileReviewG4OverlayTests.HostileReviewEntity_RoundTrip_SqlitePgSqlServer pass on LAB-OMARCHY through the temporary instance and on PAYTON-LEGION2 through LocalDB (PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 B11). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-003 [original satisfied=False]: RecoveryRunDeletedAtAssertion_AcceptsDatetime2UtcRoundTripAndRejectsOffsetColumnOrShiftedInstant, then SqlServer_RequirementsRecoveryRun_SoftDelete_RoundTripsUtcDeletedAt: on a clean SQL Server database migrated to head, RequirementsRecoveryRuns.DeletedAtUtc is datetime2 (the AddHostileReviewTables AlterColumn) and a soft-deleted recovery run reads back its DeletedAtUtc as the same UTC instant (TR-MCP-DB-DTO-001; PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 B8, triage-report-71d801ad). The gate accepts a datetime2 column whose read-back equals the written instant at offset zero, and rejects a datetimeoffset column, a shifted instant, a non-zero offset and a missing value. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Test TEST-MCP-DB-006. Original obligation: Validates TR-MCP-DB-006. The keyed async lock unit tests, which cite this record: one asserts a second same-key acquire stays pending (Task.WhenAny vs a 250ms delay) until the first is disposed, then completes; another asserts a different-key acquire completes while the first key is held. The pair discriminates a correct keyed lock from a no-op (fails the same-key case) and a global lock (fails the different-key case) - red-verified against a no-op stub, green after implementing per-key reference-counted SemaphoreSlim. The session-log replace and delete unit tests, which cite this record, confirm DeleteTurnAsync correctness is preserved under the lock (AC2).

Pester: a supersede where the server holds a refined title and the local cache holds only the hook's raw first line preserves the refined title, and a supersede with no local title still persists. Pester: failTurn closes a turn that exists only in the plugin cache with an empty in-process REPL state, where it previously returned "No active session exists". Unit, client: serializing each session-log request body through the real McpClientJsonContext resolver succeeds, and the audit-driven cases cover every request body found in the typed client rather than only the three known sites; before the fix the anonymous bodies throw NotSupportedException from GetTypeInfo. Unit, server: with an unreachable store the health path no longer reports plain Healthy while persistence is dead, the trust nonce still echoes exactly, and a backend-unavailable condition surfaces as one typed error across REST and the MCP tools instead of four different shapes.

### TR-MCP-AUDIT-004

Disposition: Amended to current direction; implementation pending. Owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph6/Ph7. Coverage: U10,U13; FullProjectSessionGraphPreserved; AuditBackfillVerified.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-AUDIT-001 -> TR-MCP-AUDIT-004.
Current contract: Project history excludes secrets and automatic operational audit. This does NOT make audit local-only: every host retains permanent local audit and an append-only outbox delivers sanitized records to the hub unversioned ledger independently of project commits. AuditRecord key=(OriginNodeId,RecordId UUID); fields ProjectId,EntityKind,EntityId,OriginSequence int64,OccurredUtc,Actor,Action,PayloadVersion,PayloadHash,Payload. POST audit/batches validates token/project, inserts atomically by key and returns per-record durable receipts. Same key/hash is already_received; same key/different hash=409 audit_id_conflict. No UPDATE/DELETE permission. Local pruning is forbidden. Migration backfills TODO and generic audit history with counts/hashes and receipts. Credential values are excluded at capture, not silently replicated.

### TR-MCP-CLIENT-001

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate. Coverage: Cross-cutting obligation retained with its existing canonical test mapping; no new feature scope.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Typed client request bodies are registered for source-generated JSON. Original obligation: Implements FR-MCP-143 and closes BUG-TRIAGE-088, 093, 095, and the client half of 090, 094, and 101. McpClientBase serializes every request through the source-generated McpClientJsonContext, whose TypeInfoResolver has no JsonTypeInfo for compiler-generated anonymous types. src/QBrainAi.Client/SessionLogClient.cs nevertheless posts anonymous bodies at line 160 (session lifecycle open, new { title, model }) and lines 236 and 247 (SetSessionTitleAsync and SetTurnTitleAsync, new { title }), so GetTypeInfo throws NotSupportedException and those endpoints never execute. Every request body in the typed client SHALL be a declared type carrying JsonPropertyName attributes and XML documentation, and SHALL be registered in McpClientJsonContext with JsonSerializable. The whole client SHALL be audited for the same pattern rather than only the three known sites, because this defect class was reported five separate times, and the audit result SHALL be reported. Coverage SHALL exercise the real McpClientJsonContext serialization path rather than a hand-rolled JsonSerializer call, so the test fails for the same reason production did.
**Covered by:** FR: FR-MCP-143; TEST: TEST-MCP-194, TEST-MCP-REPL-040
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: Workspace foreign-key integrity. Original obligation: Every persistent table with WorkspaceId must have a canonical Workspaces foreign key with Restrict or NoAction delete behavior. The FK is required for non-memory tables, including global rows represented by the reserved empty WorkspaceId row and federation mappings. MemoryEntity and MemoryIndexEntity are explicit scope exceptions: Global rows have null WorkspaceId under TR-MCP-MEMORY-001 and the memory index contract; Workspace rows have non-null WorkspaceId, which must reference Workspaces. The index workspace stamp must match its parent memory.

- PAYTON-LEGION2/ac-tr-mcp-db-002-memory-optional-fk [original satisfied=False]: MemoryEntity and MemoryIndexEntity have optional canonical Workspaces FKs that reject orphan non-null WorkspaceId values and do not cascade on workspace deletion. Source LAB-OMARCHY: Concurrent turn deletes serialized per session. Original obligation: Concurrent sessionlog_delete_turn operations on a single session SHALL be serialized through a process-wide per-session gate so a burst (observed: 13 parallel deletes) cannot exhaust or poison the SQL Server connection pool; deletes on different sessions SHALL still run concurrently; the deployed SqlClient ConnectRetryCount=6/ConnectRetryInterval=10s defaults SHALL be preserved. Acceptance Criteria: (AC1) acquiring the per-session lock for the same key blocks a second acquirer until release, while a different key acquires without blocking (KeyedAsyncLock); (AC2) DeleteTurnAsync continues to soft-delete a turn and refresh the parent turn count correctly under the lock. Origin: BUG-TRIAGE-079 (concurrent gated deletes poisoned the pool with SqlClient error 19 then 40 for ~3 minutes while SQL Server stayed up). Note: pool poisoning is a SQL-Server-runtime property not unit-reproducible; the deterministic serialization proof is the KeyedAsyncLock unit test, with SessionLogService.DeleteTurnAsync wrapped in the per-session lock keyed by sourceType/sessionId. Validated by TEST-MCP-DB-006.
**Covered by:** FR: FR-MCP-105; TEST: TEST-MCP-138, TEST-MCP-139, TEST-MCP-140, TEST-MCP-DB-006
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

Acceptance Criteria: (AC1) acquiring the per-session lock for the same key blocks a second acquirer until release, while a different key acquires without blocking (KeyedAsyncLock); (AC2) DeleteTurnAsync continues to soft-delete a turn and refresh the parent turn count correctly under the lock. Source LAB-OMARCHY: DateTimeOffset values are stored as UTC DateTime on every provider. Original obligation: McpDbContext stores every DateTimeOffset property as a UTC DateTime through DateTimeOffsetToUtcDateTimeConverter, applied to all DateTimeOffset properties in ConfigureConventions, so the SQLite provider can translate timestamp predicates and ordering to SQL; every timestamp in the schema is UTC, so the conversion is lossless. Each provider's migrations create the columns the converted model maps; on SQL Server that is datetime2 (RequirementsRecoveryRuns.DeletedAtUtc was created as datetimeoffset and corrected by AddHostileReviewTables, PLAN-SESSIONTRIAGEFIX-001 B8).
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-001 [original satisfied=False]: For SQLite, PostgreSQL and SQL Server, the model configured the way startup configures it has no pending changes against that provider's migrations, so every DateTimeOffset column the migrations create matches the converted model (ProviderMigrationModelTests.ProviderMigrations_HaveNoPendingModelChanges). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-002 [original satisfied=False]: On SQL Server, RequirementsRecoveryRuns.DeletedAtUtc is datetime2 and a soft-deleted recovery run reads back its DeletedAtUtc as the same UTC instant (TEST-MCP-102, SqlServer_RequirementsRecoveryRun_SoftDelete_RoundTripsUtcDeletedAt). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Hub Proxy Federation Contract. Original obligation: Federation configuration SHALL include Role, HubBaseUrl, ProxyId, EnrollmentToken, queue settings, and sync settings while preserving existing target/route configuration. Durable storage SHALL track proxies, proxy-hosted workspaces, operations, outbox fanout rows, and conflicts across SQLite, PostgreSQL, and SQL Server providers. Hub endpoints SHALL support proxy enrollment, heartbeat, proxy/workspace inventory, operation intake, acknowledgement, queue status, conflicts, sync, and adapter coverage. LocalProxy routing SHALL forward MCP traffic to the hub with loop-protection and operation headers, while local infrastructure and federation diagnostic endpoints remain local. Mutating LocalProxy requests SHALL queue durably when the hub is unreachable and replay through the hub intake endpoint.
**Covered by:** FR: FR-MCP-103; TEST: TEST-MCP-136, TEST-MCP-MEMORY-FED-001

Current contract: Use TR-MCP-FEDERATION-014 seed in Ph3 and join after review/publication in Ph6. Audit delivery follows TR-MCP-AUDIT-004. Manifest preserves typed values, logical IDs/aliases, versions, timestamps and full session graph. Per-table stable keyset batches and immutable source fingerprint support resume. Import never fans out original commands; only the reviewed version commit may publish.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: Workspace-scoped adapter apply, bulk enumerate, backfill envelopes. Original obligation: Hub-side IFederationStateAdapter.ApplyAsync runs inside a workspace scope set from the operation GlobalWorkspaceId (OverrideWorkspaceId on the scoped McpDbContext), for every adapter, not only memory. Adapters gain EnumerateAsync(workspace, cursor, batchSize) for bulk export. A proxy-side FederationBackfillService runs after first successful enrollment, iterates enrolled workspaces x domains with a durable per-workspace/domain cursor, and sends batched FederationExecutionEnvelopes with applyMode backfill (no fanout rows, no echo, BaseVersion null, preserved ids/timestamps). New adapters cover use cases, documents/chunks, triage, brain slots, handoff runs and actors, or the domain is declared local-only with a reason. FederationStatus exposes backfill state; FederationMiddleware serves reads locally for workspaces whose backfill is incomplete (D4). A parity report compares row counts and checksums per domain per workspace.

Current contract: Route the existing memory API locally. Version workspace-scoped memory rows and version graph through the Dolt project context; keep global/machine memory in operational storage. Validate memory identity and scope on local writes and merge. No separate replay adapter is required for Dolt mode; retain explicit legacy adapter compatibility only.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Memory Federation Adapter Contract. Original obligation: Memory federation SHALL register a memory state adapter that snapshots active memory rows by globally unique memory ID and applies signed REST-originated memory operations. The adapter SHALL preserve memory ID, scope, workspace ownership, category, raw text, timestamps, soft-delete semantics, and version tokens based on MemoryEntity.Version. Workspace-scoped memory rows SHALL only apply when the operation GlobalWorkspaceId matches the row owner. LocalProxy queueing SHALL accept POST /mcpserver/memory only when the JSON body supplies an explicit valid MEMORY-* ID, and SHALL accept PUT, PATCH, and DELETE /mcpserver/memory/{id} as replayable memory operations.
**Covered by:** FR: FR-MCP-103, FR-MCP-MEMORY-008; TEST: TEST-MCP-136, TEST-MCP-MEMORY-FED-001

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Single request-path normalization entry point. Original obligation: Introduce a single request-path normalization entry point (IWorkspacePathNormalizer backed by WorkspaceIdentityPath) with an injectable host-platform abstraction; all resolvers listed in section 1.2 use it. No direct Path.GetFullPath on request-supplied workspace paths outside it (enforced by an architecture test).
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC1 [original satisfied=False]: IWorkspacePathNormalizer.Combine treats a segment with one leading backslash as root-relative on Windows: it keeps the current root's volume, the drive (C:/) or the UNC server and share (//srv/share/), and replaces the rest of the path, on a Linux hub and a Windows host (PR 81 finding P12, PR 82 review). Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: Single request-path normalizer with injectable host platform. Original obligation: Introduce one request-path normalization entry point (IWorkspacePathNormalizer backed by WorkspaceIdentityPath) with an injectable host-platform abstraction so tests can simulate a Linux host on Windows. Every resolver that receives a request-supplied workspace path uses it: TriageService.ResolveSubmittingWorkspace/ResolveEffectiveWorkspaceAsync and query methods, WorkspaceService.NormalizePath (GetAsync/Create/Init), WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware.PopulateContext, HandoffWorkspacePaths, QBrainAiMcpTools.ApplyWorkspaceOverride, WorkspaceServiceAccessor, HandoffIngestionService, WorkspaceTokenService.Normalize, TodoServiceResolver, TodoServiceFactory, EfTodoService, RequirementsDatabaseDocumentService.CreateScope/TryGetRequestWorkspacePath, HostileReviewService, TodoExecutionService, FileGitHubWorkspaceTokenStore, AgentPoolService.BuildPooledDeviceId, AuditedAgentCliClient, RepoFileService, MarkerFileService, WikiDumpService, WorkspacePolicyDirectiveParser, AgentHelpPinnedPathResolver, HandoffSourceResolver, SessionLogWorkspaceAttributionValidator. Path.GetFullPath is applied only to host-native paths. An architecture test fails on any new direct Path.GetFullPath over request-supplied workspace paths outside an explicit allowlist.

Source PAYTON-LEGION2: Federation-aware workspace resolution on the hub. Original obligation: WorkspaceResolutionMiddleware gains a federation lookup before path lookup: when X-Mcp-Proxy-Id is present and authenticated by the inter-server hub token, resolve FederationWorkspaces by (ProxyId, normalized proxy-local path) or by GlobalWorkspaceId from X-Workspace-Path or X-Mcp-Global-Workspace-Id, then load the canonical Workspaces row by CanonicalWorkspaceId. Requests with X-Mcp-Federation-Hop and no resolvable workspace return 4xx (no API-key default fallback). The hub token is scoped to the proxy's registered workspaces. FederationMiddleware on the LocalProxy sends the hub GlobalWorkspaceId (cached from enrollment) in X-Mcp-Global-Workspace-Id.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Session-log federation snapshot carries the full session graph. Original obligation: SessionLogFederationStateAdapter.SnapshotAsync SHALL return, for an existing session of the local store, a payload in the UnifiedSessionLogDto shape that the adapter's ApplyAsync accepts, carrying every session field and every turn with all of its fields and child collections (actions, tags, context items, processing dialog, commits with their files, design decisions, requirements discovered, files modified, blockers), equal to what the local session-log read returns for that session after the outbound session-log sanitizer. Building a snapshot reads only the local store. The snapshot version stays the session's explicit version (ContentHash, else LastUpdated in Unix milliseconds, else TurnCount).
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-001 [original satisfied=False]: For a session whose turn carries a value in every turn field and at least one item in every child collection (commits with files), the snapshot payload deserializes as UnifiedSessionLogDto and equals the local session-log read of that session field by field, child collections in order. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph6/Ph7.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-002 [original satisfied=False]: A snapshot payload is applyable: applying it through the adapter's ApplyAsync to a store without that session and then reading the session there yields a DTO equal to the source read, for values the sanitizer leaves unchanged. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph6/Ph7.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-003 [original satisfied=False]: A value the outbound session-log sanitizer redacts in the session-log read is redacted the same way in the snapshot payload. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph6/Ph7.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-004 [original satisfied=False]: Building a snapshot makes no federation data call: a session that exists only on a peer is not snapshotted, and a local session's snapshot carries only local turns. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph6/Ph7.
- LAB-OMARCHY/AC-005 [original satisfied=False]: A missing or soft-deleted session yields PayloadJson "null" and Version null, and an existing session's Version equals its explicit version (ContentHash, else LastUpdated in Unix milliseconds, else TurnCount), as before this requirement. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph6/Ph7.

POST control/poll is read-only. POST control/report includes all epochs and observed generation; stale worker=409 stale_worker_epoch, stale hub=409 hub_epoch_changed. Poll every30s with20% jitter; commit/reconnect/on-demand wake immediately. Backoff1s doubling to60s. Generation ordering is valid only within HubEpoch. Restore generates a new non-reused random epoch before listeners reopen.

Current contract: Review record key=ReviewId UUID; fields ProjectId,Branch,baseHeads[],localHead,incomingHead,candidateRef,conflictingCells[],validationErrors[],createdUtc,lastNotifiedUtc,resolution,actor. GET conflicts and status expose it. Resolve binds exact heads and returns409 stale_review if changed. Safe TODO policy: identical concurrent values collapse; edits to different cells may merge only if invariants pass; duplicate immutable child IDs deduplicate only when bytes agree. Status, priority, title, description, dependency deletions and same-cell disagreements always require review; no timestamp last-writer-wins. Notify immediately in status and durable operator inbox; warn at15min, escalate at24h, repeat at most hourly; no email/Slack sent implicitly. Same-branch dependent commits wait; unrelated branches/projects continue.

Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-181 -> TR-MCP-FEDERATION-008.
Current contract: OperationReceipt binds requestId, node, project, branch, hubEpoch, candidate/result hashes and outcome. Durable refs pin candidates/reviews before receipt completion. Worker claims are fenced by WorkerEpoch; reconnect never trusts stored generation across HubEpoch. Fetch may advance remote-tracking refs, never dirty live refs. Restore first disables ingress, restores compatible data/control/audit checkpoints, creates new epoch, then re-advertises verified heads; node-held missing commits are uploaded and ancestry-validated before normal synchronization resumes.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: recovery without lost or repeated work contract. Original obligation: Server: durable operation states, worker-epoch fencing, idempotent receipts, restart reconstruction and schema compatibility barriers. Plugin: resume/status/cancel without false rollback claims. Implement through the typed server/client/plugin contracts and storage boundaries in the Dolt federation architecture; preserve existing explicit legacy modes. Covered by FR-MCP-181 and TEST-MCP-229.

### TR-MCP-FEDERATION-010

Disposition: Amended to current direction; implementation pending. Owner: Ph7. Coverage: U01,U02,U05,U07,U08,U11,U12,U13,U14,U15.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-183 -> TR-MCP-FEDERATION-010.
Current contract: All plugins call typed server/client/MCP/REPL contracts; no raw SQL/CLI fallback. Status DTO includes projectId, branch, localHead, publishedHead, observedUtc, dirty, ahead, behind, publicationState, incomingTarget, connectivity, reviewCount, oldestReviewAgeSeconds, waitingLocalWorkAgeSeconds, auditPendingCount, hubEpoch, workerEpoch. Capability contract version1 is negotiated; unknown version=409 unsupported_contract. Administrative maintenance.preflight/attest/status and migration commands share the same typed service as Nuke. Stable error envelope={code, message, requestId, projectId, branch, retryable, details}; status codes are locked in the technical contract appendix.

Technical contract appendix (TR-MCP-FEDERATION-010): all below routes are under /qbrainai/federation unless the commits route specifies the project/branch path. Every mutation requires requestId UUID and authenticated subject; node network requests additionally carry the project-scoped reference token. Responses carry contractVersion=1 and the stable error envelope. Path/body ProjectId mismatch is403 unauthorized_project. No endpoint accepts credentials in URL, argv or persisted receipts.

Read/status routes: GET capabilities returns200 {contractVersion,capabilities}; GET projects/{projectId}/status returns200 the status DTO; GET projects/{projectId}/branches returns200 {items:[{name,head,dirty,publishedHead}]}; GET conflicts and GET conflicts/{reviewId} return200 review records filtered by membership. POST control/poll returns200 {hubEpoch,subscriptions}; reads never acknowledge work. POST control/report validates observed generation and worker epoch, returns200 the durable receipt or409 the named fence error. Poll/report use control scope; reads use read scope.

Mutation routes: POST enroll returns201 the enrollment (or200 the same idempotent enrollment); POST projects/{projectId}/branches creates201 {branch,head}; POST projects/{projectId}/branches/select returns200 {sessionId,branch,head} after local lease validation; POST projects/{projectId}/branches/{branch}/commits returns201 {commitHash,publicationState}, or200 the same commit on retry. POST publication/accept returns200 {requestId,hubEpoch,publishedHead,publishedGeneration,receiptId} only after the guarded ref update is durable. POST branches/merge and POST conflicts/{reviewId}/resolve bind expected heads and return201 the new commit; a conflict returns202 {outcome:needs_review,reviewId} while the live head stays unchanged. Share/merge/publication require publish scope plus branch permission. Enrollment requires enroll scope and the operator owner-binding approval.

POST audit/batches accepts {requestId,projectId,records:[AuditRecord]}, returns200 {receipts:[{originNodeId,recordId,payloadHash,outcome,receivedUtc}]}, and requires audit scope. Every record must match the token/project and batch ProjectId. Any invalid record rejects the whole batch before insert. Exact duplicate is already_received; payload mismatch is409 audit_id_conflict. POST ids/reservations accepts {requestId,projectId,entityKind,humanId,nodeId}, requires publish scope and returns201 the reservation or200 its exact retry; another owner gets409 human_id_reserved. POST maintenance/preflight and POST maintenance/attest, GET maintenance/status and POST migration/{plan|dry-run|run|resume|verify|export-todos|cutover|abort}, GET migration/status are operator-only local service operations. They are exposed through typed clients and Nuke, never public node-admin capabilities. A long migration returns202 {migrationId,status}; status/verify return200 a receipt, and a denied readiness check never performs cutover.

Stable failure mapping:401 invalid_token;403 unauthorized_project;409 head_changed, working_set_changed, lease_lost, branch_busy, stale_review, stale_worker_epoch, hub_epoch_changed, ambiguous_legacy_id, human_id_reserved, human_id_unreserved, target_not_empty, maintenance_not_ready, migration_source_changed, migration_verification_failed, incompatible_schema, unsupported_contract and publication_required;422 secret_detected, dependency_cycle and unsupported_provider;501 unsupported_capability;503 identity_unavailable or remote_unavailable. TLS rejection produces the local transport code tls_peer_untrusted with no HTTP response and no request sent. Missing required fields return400 invalid_request. Missing authorized resources return404 not_found; project authorization precedes existence disclosure. Malformed/unsupported token is401; valid token lacking project rights is403.

Outcome fields are not HTTP errors: committed_locally, already_received, waiting_local_work and needs_review remain visible through200 status/report responses. A requested live mutation blocked by drafts returns409 waiting_local_work; an integration that durably creates review returns202 needs_review. dependency_cycle is a validationErrors entry on that review as well as422 for a directly invalid local mutation. retryable=true is restricted to transient503/transport failures; all stale-head/fence/review errors require reread and a fresh validated action. Idempotent retries with the same requestId but different payload return409 request_id_conflict. No automatic retry creates a different commit.

Test fixtures capture actual content hashes as symbols C0,A1,B1,H1,M1,M2,S1 rather than hardcoding unstable engine hashes. Each symbol is immutable within the run manifest. Given/When/Then compares exact hash equality, specified parent sets, row values, status codes and error codes against that manifest; a successful result must never be inferred solely from an empty queue. Capture UTC timestamps and a fake clock for exact15-minute/24-hour age boundaries. All plan phase IDs use Ph0-Ph8; priority values remain separate.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: first-party plugin federation contract contract. Original obligation: Server/client: typed REST/MCP/REPL parity, normalized errors, capabilities and accurate local/published/received status, ahead/behind and stale-branch visibility. Plugins: audited status/diff/history/commit/sync/branch/review and authorized migration commands without raw SQL fallbacks or implicit commits. Implement through the typed server/client/plugin contracts and storage boundaries in the Dolt federation architecture; preserve existing explicit legacy modes. Covered by FR-MCP-183 and TEST-MCP-231.

- LAB-OMARCHY/ac-OldServerReportsUnsupportedCapability [original satisfied=False]: Prove OldServerReportsUnsupportedCapability with positive and negative-control evidence under the linked architecture contract. Disposition: Rewritten as exact GWT under the same criterion ID. Validation owner: Ph7.

### TR-MCP-FEDERATION-011

Disposition: Amended to current direction; implementation pending. Owner: Ph2/Ph3/Ph5/Ph7. Coverage: U10,U13.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-184 -> TR-MCP-FEDERATION-011.
Current contract: Project history excludes secrets and automatic operational audit. This does NOT make audit local-only: every host retains permanent local audit and an append-only outbox delivers sanitized records to the hub unversioned ledger independently of project commits. AuditRecord key=(OriginNodeId,RecordId UUID); fields ProjectId,EntityKind,EntityId,OriginSequence int64,OccurredUtc,Actor,Action,PayloadVersion,PayloadHash,Payload. POST audit/batches validates token/project, inserts atomically by key and returns per-record durable receipts. Same key/hash is already_received; same key/different hash=409 audit_id_conflict. No UPDATE/DELETE permission. Local pruning is forbidden. Migration backfills TODO and generic audit history with counts/hashes and receipts. Credential values are excluded at capture, not silently replicated.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: portable data versus host-local state contract. Original obligation: Server: complete entity/writer manifest; clone only authorized project data; keep automatic session capture, permanent local audit, secrets, controls, machine configuration and derived indexes outside shared histories; explicitly promote sanitized session snapshots at commit preview. Plugin: keep agent identity and host context separate from branchable content. Implement through the typed server/client/plugin contracts and storage boundaries in the Dolt federation architecture; preserve existing explicit legacy modes. Covered by FR-MCP-184 and TEST-MCP-232.

### TR-MCP-FEDERATION-012

Disposition: Amended to current direction; implementation pending. Owner: Ph0/Ph2/Ph5. Coverage: U01,U06,U13.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-185 -> TR-MCP-FEDERATION-012.
Current contract: Reuse existing Duende IdentityServer. Provision client fed.node.{NodeId} per single-user node, bound to ownerSubjectId through authenticated operator enrollment. Client credentials issue short-lived reference access tokens; audience qbrain-federation and scopes fed.project.{ProjectId}.{read|publish|control|audit|enroll}; protected actions additionally require server membership/branch permission. Gateway introspects without positive caching for each new request and rechecks before publication commit; unavailable identity returns503 identity_unavailable. Revoke through IdentityServer: disable client and remove its persisted reference grants. Gateway rejects inactive tokens401 invalid_token; do not invent a revocation ledger. Native remotesapi uses gateway-held credentials over loopback; clients cannot access it directly. Ph0 proves token injection/relay including redirects/object URLs, project isolation and no public-ref bypass.

Export is not full-database reverse migration. Cutover uses a verified receipt, explicit DEPLOY HOLD release and one-node soak before the second host.

### TR-MCP-HEALTH-003

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5. Coverage: U01,U06; DifferentHostPathsSameProject; UnauthorizedProjectDenied.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-143 -> TR-MCP-HEALTH-003.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Health reflects storage reachability and backend errors are typed. Original obligation: Implements FR-MCP-143 and closes BUG-TRIAGE-096. GET /health is liveness-only (tags live: self plus FederationUpstreamHealthCheck) with no storage connectivity check, so when the database backend is unreachable the server still answers Healthy and echoes the trust nonce correctly while every persistence call fails. Observed live on 2026-07-20 when the SQL host was powered off: marker trust passed, then todo_list returned a raw SqlClient "Named Pipes Provider, error: 40" string, sessionlog_query returned a misleading "transient failure, consider EnableRetryOnFailure" hint, triage_report returned a bare "an error occurred", and the plugin wrapper returned an opaque 30-second timeout, so no caller could tell that storage was simply down. The server SHALL expose storage reachability on the health path, either by degrading the status or by an explicit storage field, WITHOUT breaking the nonce echo that marker trust bootstrap depends on. Backend-unavailable conditions SHALL surface as one typed error consistently across REST and the MCP tools, distinguishable from a malformed request. Coverage SHALL simulate an unreachable store and assert the misleading Healthy result before the fix.
**Covered by:** FR: FR-MCP-143; TEST: TEST-MCP-194, TEST-MCP-REPL-040
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Identifier Naming Validation. Original obligation: `TodoValidator` SHALL validate persisted TODO IDs against the canonical regex set `^[A-Z][A-Z0-9]*(?:-[A-Z0-9]+)+-\d{3}$` or `^ISSUE-\d+$` for create/update dependency paths across all configured TODO storage providers (`yaml` and `database` per TR-MCP-TODO-005). `ISSUE-NEW` SHALL remain a create-time alias handled before persistence, not a persisted TODO identifier. `SessionLogIdentifierValidator` SHALL validate session/request IDs using canonical timestamped patterns and enforce exact source-type prefix parity (`SessionId` starts with `{sourceType}-` or `{agent}-`). Invalid values return HTTP 400 at controller boundaries and `ArgumentException` for direct service invocation.

**Covered by:** `TodoValidator`, `TodoService`, `EfTodoService`, `TodoCreationService`, `SessionLogIdentifierValidator`, `SessionLogController`, `SessionLogService`

### TR-MCP-PATHNORM-001

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5. Coverage: U01,U06; DifferentHostPathsSameProject; UnauthorizedProjectDenied.
Existing canonical links: FR-MCP-PATHNORM-001 -> TR-MCP-PATHNORM-001.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Forward-slash storage keys, rekey maintenance command and startup guard. Original obligation: WorkspaceIdentityPath.NormalizeLexicalPath emits '/' for Windows paths (UNC keeps leading '//'; \\?\ device prefixes stripped to drive or // form); BuildStorageKey becomes windows:C:/WORKSPACES/EXAMPLE style. IsRemoteWindowsPath, IsNormalizedPathWithinRoot and NormalizeLocalAdministrativeShareAlias accept both separators. A ToNativePath helper produces backslash paths only for Win32 CreateFile/GetFinalPathNameByHandle, \\?\ long-path prefixes and cmd.exe arguments. Persisted data is rewritten by an explicit maintenance command --workspace-path-rekey (decision D5, 2026-10-07) modelled on McpDatabaseEncryptionTransitionCommand: required backup unless --dry-run, report with old->new mapping and before/after counts; one transaction per database with foreign keys deferred or suspended and fully re-validated (decision D6: SQLite defer_foreign_keys, SQL Server NOCHECK then WITH CHECK CHECK, PostgreSQL DEFERRABLE + SET CONSTRAINTS ALL DEFERRED); set-based UPDATE per workspace column from a mapping table limited to Windows-shaped values; C# recomputation of hashed values (federation GlobalWorkspaceId/CanonicalWorkspaceId and hub hash-keyed Workspaces rows, TriageGroups GroupKey/GroupId with repointed children, HandoffIngestionRuns ReplayIdentity); FileGitHubWorkspaceTokenStore keys rewritten. No legacy dual-key lookup (decision D7): a startup guard refuses to start while any Windows-shaped workspace id still contains a backslash. Enrolled proxies are re-enrolled once after deploy (the Windows proxy in the original deployment). Plan: docs/plans/2026-10-07-federation-fixes.md section 4c.
- LAB-OMARCHY/p15-policy-a [original satisfied=False]: Reuse P1.4 Windows lexical normalization, preserve case, and reject distinct old keys converging before any database mutation. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/p15-safety [original satisfied=False]: Apply requires successful verified backup; all database changes and FK validation occur in one transaction; repeat apply is a no-op. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/p15-preview [original satisfied=False]: Dry-run inventory and planning run only on a database copy; report mappings, table and workspace counts, and retained manual triage hashes without secrets. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/p15-guard [original satisfied=False]: HTTP and STDIO startup refuse legacy Windows workspace ids and point to --workspace-path-rekey. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.
- LAB-OMARCHY/p15-derived [original satisfied=False]: Recompute only derivable triage, handoff, and federation identities; repoint children; preserve manual triage hashes and rewrite token-store keys with file backup. Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Validation owner: Ph0/Ph1/Ph2/Ph5.

### TR-MCP-REPL-018

Disposition: Retain compatible contract/regression obligation; no silent deletion. Owner: Ph0 inventory; affected Ph1-Ph8 regression gate. Coverage: Cross-cutting obligation retained with its existing canonical test mapping; no new feature scope.

Source LAB-OMARCHY: Turn upsert accepts a deliberately empty title. Original obligation: Implements FR-MCP-143 and closes BUG-TRIAGE-087, 089, 091, 098, the PowerShell half of 090 and 094, and the PowerShell half of 101. plugins/core/lib-ps/repl-invoke.ps1 declares the turn-upsert Title parameter as [Parameter(Mandatory)][string] with no [AllowEmptyString()] at line 944, while callers deliberately pass an empty string per TR-MCP-REPL-015 so a stale cached title is not resubmitted. PowerShell rejects the bind before any request is made, so appendDialog without a queryTitle, appendActions, completeTurn, and the supersede path all fail with a mandatory-parameter binding error rather than persisting. The parameter SHALL accept an empty string, and every call path that legitimately passes one SHALL persist. Any other mandatory string parameter on the same path that would reject a legitimate empty value SHALL be corrected the same way. Pester coverage SHALL prove the binding failure before the fix and the successful persist after, using a temporary cache directory so no test touches the real workspace state.
**Covered by:** FR: FR-MCP-143; TEST: TEST-MCP-194, TEST-MCP-REPL-040
No structured AC in this source. Its description (including any inline AC labels) remains an explicit obligation, with the disposition above. No passing criterion is invented.
